observed and bound only as `entry_count` plus SHA-256 of the UTF-8 compact JSON array of direct
entry names sorted lexicographically, with no trailing newline. It is an unattributed, immutable

npm `11.13.0` source `lib/utils/log-file.js`, SHA-256
`0545402f1a53075726505829f4dc6b0ec2f43e857fd559b8868c60feae1c46a4`, opens no logfile when

`1b851f0ef48a7925b665b4b61888619e6ce63ef92325877473de5d193d909785`, states that
`loglevel=silent` plus `logs-max=0` prevents terminal and filesystem logs. The task-log directory
must therefore remain exactly empty before and after every npm invocation: count `0` and SHA-256
`4f53cda18c2baa0c0354bb5f9a3ecbe5ed12ab4d8e11ba873c2f11161202b945` of compact JSON `[]`.
Any entry, hash drift or noncanonical/mode/type mismatch stops before the next gate.

`npm ci --ignore-scripts` blocks both and only lock-recorded install scripts: nonoptional
`esbuild@0.28.1` and optional `fsevents@2.3.3`. No npm lifecycle child may run. `fsevents` remains
optional and every Vitest argv uses `run`, never watch mode.

must contain mandatory `external_log_entry_count_before`, `external_log_entry_set_sha256_before`,
`external_log_entry_count_after`, `external_log_entry_set_sha256_after`,
`task_log_entry_count_before`, `task_log_entry_set_sha256_before`,
`task_log_entry_count_after` and `task_log_entry_set_sha256_after`, together with canonical cwd,
exact argv/environment binding and exit. External count and hash must equal the newly bound
preflight baseline on both sides; any mismatch stops before the next gate. Task-log enumeration is
confined to the canonical task log directory, rejects symlinks/non-regular entries/escape and
uses the same compact-JSON sorted-name encoding while recording only count/set hash, never raw
names or contents. Any write outside the task
cache/log/config roots or any unbound task-log transition fails closed. The pre-lifecycle
`EXTERNAL_LOG_CHECK` remains a cumulative equality check for commands already completed and cannot
substitute for any command's mandatory before/after fields.

Every non-npm build, focused test, complete test, Typecheck, list-files proof, final Synthetic

The exact fail-closed Receipt contract is embedded as
`DA5_V5_ATTEMPT10_CLEANUP_RECEIPT_SCHEMA_V2` plus
`DA5_V5_ATTEMPT10_CLEANUP_CONTRACT_V2`:

- `EVIDENCE_INIT.cleanup_state_initialized` persists exact expected checkout and registration
  literal paths, null identities/mapping and state `unbound` before `WORKTREE_ADD`.
  `WORKTREE_ADD` process index 0 itself requires `cleanup_state_before`,
  `identity_binding_timing` and `cleanup_state_after` in addition to the five generic process
  fields. Successful exit requires exact checkout/registration identity objects
  (`literal_path`, `canonical_realpath`, decimal-string `device`/`inode`), exact source/tree
  worktree mapping and an increasing sequence proof persisted before every next fallible spawn.
- Cleanup-state enums are closed to `unbound`, `partial`, `bound`, `cleanup_applied`,
  `cleanup_complete` and `cleanup_residue`; only the listed transitions are valid. `unbound`
  or `partial` launches no deletion and can end only in `cleanup_residue`.
- `actual_root_results` is exactly five fixed-order objects for checkout, cache, logs, config and
  failure-only artifact, using the closed `removed|absent|preserved|residue` enum and mandatory
  literal path, expected/before/after identity, match, removal-attempt and post-absence fields.
  `actual_registration_result` separately binds the exact registration path, before/after
  identity, before/after exact worktree mapping, list membership and closed
  `removed|absent|residue` enum.
- `CLEANUP` and `POSTCLEANUP` directly require their before/after cleanup states and exact result
  objects; `POSTCLEANUP` also requires successful-artifact and evidence-preservation objects.
  `FINALIZE` directly requires named deep-equal copies of both records, final state, preservation
  objects, exact manifest flags/status and six derivation assertions.
- Manifest flags derive deterministically from the fixed POSTCLEANUP results; `PASS` is possible
  only when every flag/assertion is true and final state is `cleanup_complete`. Missing,
  undefined, extra, unordered, non-enum or unequal data and every residue force
  `FAIL_CLOSED` before receipt/manifest immutability. Undefined roots cannot be dereferenced.
  Later not-cleanup remains supplemental evidence only and cannot modify or supersede any
  immutable record.

The candidate creates no Product correctness,
authentication, tenant, network, database, timekeeping, APK, installation, ADB, device, Tag,
Hardware, Human-V5, production, production-data, deployment or distribution claim or authority.

## 2. Confirmed finding `DA5-V5-HARNESS-ARTIFACT-01`

Repository truth on the exact preparation baseline is:

- `apps/synthetic-android-e2e/package.json` starts DA5 V5 with
  `node dist/da5V5Main.js`; `dist/` is ignored by `.gitignore`.
- Corrected source `apps/synthetic-android-e2e/src/da5V5Main.ts` requires
  `TAPTIME_DA5_V5_TALKBACK_PACKAGE` and validates it through
  `requireDa5V5TalkBackPackage`.
- `apps/mobile/scripts/da5V5AndroidDevice.mjs` allowlists exactly
  `com.google.android.marvin.talkback` and
  `com.samsung.android.accessibility.talkback`; the current controller verifies the exact active
  expected package and version.
- The locally startable ignored `apps/synthetic-android-e2e/dist/da5V5Main.js` is 800,623 bytes,
  mode `0644`, SHA-256
  `f0f4fdde80f7cf8cc1869aaa4b2267dbf22539e240beacbddf51cd3591e59cac`, and has modification
  time `2026-07-28T18:11:16+0200`, before source commit `a0359a8` on
  `2026-07-31T16:30:50+0200`.
- Static inspection of that ignored bundle finds the Google package twice, the Samsung package
  zero times, `TAPTIME_DA5_V5_TALKBACK_PACKAGE` zero times and `talkBackPackage` zero times.

Therefore the reviewed source correction remains valid source evidence, but the currently
startable ignored bundle is stale and Google-only. It is not an authorized execution artifact and
cannot support the documented Google-or-Samsung pre-run closure. Product Human V5 remains
**DO NOT START**.

GitHub Actions run `30638926835`, attempt 1, is correctly bound to `a0359a8` and completed
successfully with 12/12 jobs. It proves the tracked source candidate, not the later ignored local
bundle. Earlier ADO references to 11/11 are corrected by this R0 synchronization.

  source/artifact manifest.
- **Independent review:** exact source commit/tree, exact ADO delta, locked dependency state,
  Node binding, bundle/source-map SHA-256, focused results and omissions. Verdict is only
  `APPROVED` or `CHANGES REQUIRED`, with P0–P3 findings; approval requires zero open P0–P3.
- **V5:** not authorized and not run by this closure.

that the exact reviewed bundle closure includes both allowlisted packages, the explicit expected

task-owned output and leave the repository without an authorized startable DA5-V5 bundle. The

success, synchronize the exact read-only bundle/manifest/Node/dependency/review bindings and keep
all Product Human V5 fields `UNBOUND — DO NOT START` until a separate Human authorization.

### Typecheck-membership receipt correction

For both `MOBILE_TYPECHECK` and `SYNTHETIC_TYPECHECK`, process array index 1 captures stdout
memory-only under `DA5_V5_ATTEMPT11_TYPECHECK_MEMBERSHIP_RECEIPT_SCHEMA_V1`. Its exact ordered
pipeline is: bounded byte capture and pre-decode overflow at byte 16,777,217; fatal UTF-8 decode
with explicit BOM rejection; pre-split NUL/bare-CR rejection and LF-only split; one exact final
empty-segment removal only for terminal LF plus validated CRLF handling; memory-only logical line
count with maximum 131,072 and saturation 131,073 before path work; then canonical path validation,
UTF-8-byte deduplication/sort, compact-JSON digest and membership. Exact zero-byte decoded stdout
yields zero logical lines and the empty normalized set. Leading/consecutive LF retains an empty
segment and fails malformed after the line-limit decision. Raw output, raw/split line counts and the
normalized list are never persisted.

Every executed gate record directly requires `result` with exactly
`normalization_status`, `failure_code`, `stdout_bytes`, `listed_file_count`,
`normalized_set_sha256`, `required_test_path`, `observed_match`, `required_test_included` and
`raw_list_preserved`. `listed_file_count` is exactly the cardinality of the final deduplicated,
normalized, UTF-8-byte-sorted canonical repo-relative path set serialized for the digest; duplicate
input lines never increase it. Complete parsing preserves that count, digest and membership decision
even on gate failure. Parser/capture failure uses the closed precedence and failure-code enum, null
final-set count/digest/match, false membership and `raw_list_preserved:false`. The expected Mobile and
Synthetic paths are bound to their exact tracked blob/file/tsconfig hashes in the map. A
Typecheck gate passes if and only if both mapped processes exit 0 and the exact required test is
in the normalized set. Every other executed outcome is a closed failure with the same result
schema; every unexecuted later gate remains individually omitted.

All Attempt-10 evidence-first, external-log, lifecycle, omission, precleanup, Cleanup Schema V2,
Cleanup Contract V2, postcleanup, immutable evidence and FINALIZE requirements remain binding
after the mechanical Attempt-11 substitution. No Product correctness, authentication, tenant,
network, database, timekeeping, APK, installation, ADB, device, Tag, Hardware, Human-V5,
production, production-data, deployment or distribution claim or authority is created.

### Next gate

/ DO NOT EXECUTE**. Publication does not authorize Hardware or Human/Product V5.

The compact JSON command map is exactly 222,596 UTF-8 bytes with no trailing newline and SHA-256
`5bc7e519d4a942f4ceed7e5a4b3a5e6dc5ecbf6d8b7ac8648616d0e0a2291a03`. Its ID is
`DA5_V5_ATTEMPT12_COMMAND_MAP_V1`. It has exactly 45 ordered gates and three npm groups. Each
external process is spawned directly from its exact argv; shell invocation remains forbidden.
The embedded Cleanup Receipt Schema V2 is exactly 84,102 compact UTF-8 bytes with no trailing
newline and SHA-256 `4caa1b43e2b99b22400ce16213bff4b890dd855b13e4caafae8829fe7ff82d94`.
The embedded reporter schema remains exactly 73,538 compact UTF-8 bytes with no trailing newline
and SHA-256 `c78b307bb5003e1d81a97dd909b9ddaeeabda4c98d1475f1a185e680cfb304a7`.

### Cleanup V2 signal-terminated WORKTREE_ADD integration and absorbing residue

`WORKTREE_ADD[0]` and `identity_binding_timing` now carry deep-equal outer/inner termination kind,
nullable exit code and nullable closed signal category. The exact partition is not-started, exit
0, positive nonzero exit or signal; raw signal values and synthesized `128+signal` exits are
forbidden. Signal termination reattests checkout identity, registration identity and exact
worktree-list mapping as `absent|exact|mismatch|ambiguous`. A fixed first-match classifier covers
all 64 tuples: all absent is unbound, all exact is bound, other absent/exact mixtures are partial,
and every mismatch or ambiguity is `cleanup_residue`.

Unbound, partial, mismatched or ambiguous signal state launches no deletion and terminates
fail-closed. Only a fully exact bound state may enter existing Cleanup-V2 removal, and then only
after the unchanged pre-removal identity and mapping revalidation. Eight focused signal fixtures
cover no materialization, checkout only, registration/mapping only, full binding, identity
mismatch, mapping mismatch, ambiguity and rejected removal without full binding. Six additional
termination fixtures cover all valid branches and invalid exit/signal collisions; Cleanup and
POSTCLEANUP terminal states are explicit for every signal fixture.

The 56 reattestation tuples containing at least one `mismatch` or `ambiguous` observation each
have an exact mechanized fixture. Binding persists `cleanup_residue`. The terminal state has no
outgoing workflow transition and cannot be repaired, rebound, resumed, promoted or used for
deletion. Only the exact `CLEANUP` and `POSTCLEANUP` receipt boundaries may retain the same status:
each increments `state_sequence` by one, preserves every other cleanup-state field and records
`removal_attempted:false` for every root and registration result. These are gate-scoped evidence
retentions, not general `cleanup_residue->cleanup_residue` transitions.

`FINALIZE` performs no transition or removal. It binds `cleanup_record_sequence` and
`postcleanup_record_sequence`, deep-copies the named immutable records, keeps
`cleanup_state_final.status=cleanup_residue`, sets both `cleanup_complete` and
`postcleanup_complete` false, and records `manifest_status=FAIL_CLOSED`. Correct copy or derivation
assertions cannot promote the terminal state.

### Bounded Vitest result normalization

Gate `V2_SYNTHETIC_TEST` invokes the already locked Vitest 4.1.9 CLI exactly once with the two
additional argv elements `--reporter=json` and
`--outputFile=<CHECKOUT>/.da5-v5-attempt12-vitest-reporter/vitest-result.json`. The package-lock
entry remains unchanged. `DEPENDENCY_BINDINGS` must verify the package-lock resolved/integrity and
the checkout-contained nonsymlink package metadata, CLI entry, JSON-reporter implementation,
reporter types and CLI-option implementation by exact device, size and SHA-256 before Gate 32.
Stdout and stderr use non-forwarded pipes with separate exact 1 MiB byte counters; content is
never decoded, hashed or persisted. The first excess byte records only saturated 1 MiB + 1,
remaining bytes are drained and discarded, and the gate fails closed.

The embedded `DA5_V5_ATTEMPT12_VITEST_RESULT_RECEIPT_SCHEMA_V1` is authoritative. The reporter is
limited to 16 MiB; a 16 MiB + 1 byte probe is overflow. UTF-8 must be valid, BOM is forbidden,
JSON and every object/array/string/number/status must satisfy the closed schema and explicit
bounds, and unknown fields fail closed. Reporter root and file are checked by `lstat`, canonical
realpath, device and inode. Symlinks, non-regular files, boundary escapes, device mismatches and
unexpected entries fail closed.

The exact expected test allowlist is derived only from tracked files at the exact source commit
under `apps/synthetic-android-e2e/tests`: 13 repository-relative UTF-8 paths. Its compact
UTF-8-byte-sorted JSON array is 883 bytes and SHA-256
`6d3d0d28585a65d8e1357716285896176549416262b3fdba5e5a88ff4966716f`. The schema embeds every
path with Git blob ID, size and SHA-256, plus the exact package/config/tsconfig inputs. Every
reported file name must normalize canonically beneath the checkout to one allowlisted path.
Unknown or duplicate paths, missing members, count mismatches and exit/result contradictions fail
closed. Duplicate test identifiers are not collapsed; their bounded hashes retain multiplicity.

Only the schema's 31-field sanitized result object and 13-field reporter-binding object may enter
the receipt. Every field now has one exact type, nullability, materialized default and cross-field
rule; missing, `undefined`, duplicate and additional fields fail closed. It retains process exit,
reporter byte count when safely available,
`reporter_success`, expected and observed file-set count/digest/membership, missing/unexpected and
file/suite/test status counts, closed failure code/category, and a stable canonical failure
signature. It never persists a digest derived from raw reporter content. Safe failure categories
are deliberately ambiguous where Vitest JSON cannot prove a
narrower cause: assertion-result or test-hook; suite-hook/collection/transform/configuration;
worker/process/infrastructure; exit-result contradiction; process-not-started; or reporter/contract
failure. `none` is permitted only for
the exact pass contract.

`normalization_status` is exactly `not_started`, `capture_failed`, `decode_failed`,
`schema_failed` or `normalized`. Before full normalization, process/reporter/observed/count and
signature fields follow the status matrix's exact null rules; expected count/digest and both raw
preservation flags are always fixed. `process_termination_kind` is exactly
`not_started|exit|signal`; `process_exit_code` is nonnull exactly for `exit`, while
`process_signal_category` is nonnull exactly for `signal`. The raw signal name/number and any
synthetic `128+signal` exit value are forbidden. `reporter_bytes` is null until an exact bounded
read result exists, exact 16 MiB + 1 only
for overflow, and otherwise the exact complete byte count. A normalized tuple has all counts,
exact 13-file membership, `reporter_success` and signature nonnull.

The reporter lifecycle is the closed state machine
`preflight_not_started`, `preflight_failed`, `process_not_started`, `process_completed`,
`reporter_missing`, `read_overflow`, `read_complete`, `parsed`, `normalized`,
`cleanup_removed`, `cleanup_absent`, `cleanup_residue`. Only the embedded transitions are legal;
the last three states are terminal. Each of the 13 binding fields has exact type/null/default
semantics. Identity fields are null unless exact safe identities exist; each comparison boolean
is true/false only when its predicate was actually decided and otherwise null. A final receipt
forbids the in-memory `pending` disposition. `not_created`, `removed_before_receipt` and
`deferred_to_bound_checkout_cleanup` have exhaustive terminal tuples.

Validation-error selection first evaluates the schema's 46 uniquely ranked checks and persists
only the first applicable true check. The order is total within every bucket: bounded structural
limits precede unknown fields, `unknown_field` explicitly precedes `schema_type_mismatch`, and
path/membership/counter/signature checks follow their fixed canonical order. Twelve multi-fault
fixtures bind the exact status/code/category/nullability outcome. Category selection is then
first-match, mutually exclusive and exhaustive. Cleanup residue overrides
everything as `reporter_or_contract_failure` / `reporter_cleanup_failed`. Other
preflight/capture/read/decode/schema/path/allowlist/count/signature-contract failures use the same
category and their exact first failure code. A clean non-start is `process_not_started`. For a
fully normalized reporter, signal termination selects
`worker_process_or_infrastructure_failure_ambiguous` / `process_signal_terminated`. For an exit
termination, the contradiction is exactly `(process_exit_code === 0) !== reporter_success`;
after earlier contract predicates, failed tests
select assertion-result/test-hook ambiguity, zero failed tests plus failed file/suite selects
suite-hook/collection/transform/configuration ambiguity, and a consistent false result/nonzero
exit with zero failed test/file/suite counts selects worker/process/infrastructure ambiguity. Only
the exact success predicate selects `none`; every remaining normalized tuple is a reporter
contract failure.

The legacy-named `failure_signature_sha256` is now a normalized-result signature: it is nonnull
for every `normalized` tuple, including PASS and exit contradiction, and null for every earlier
normalization status. It hashes only the closed canonical versioned sanitized object, allowlisted
normalized paths,
counts, closed process-termination kind/exit/signal category and bounded SHA-256 identifier hashes.
Raw signal, JSON, raw stdout/stderr, names,
titles, messages, failure messages, stacks, timestamps, durations, metadata, tags, arbitrary
paths and secrets are never evidence and must never be written to receipt, snapshot, manifest or
artifact. The reporter raw file is never copied. After memory-only normalization it is removed
before the Gate-32 receipt; only if its exact recorded identity cannot be removed safely may its
cleanup be deferred to the already bound Gate-43 checkout cleanup. Such deferral cannot pass the
gate or `FINALIZE`; the snapshot still excludes the reporter and complete Cleanup V2 absence is
required.

If cleanup becomes residue after successful normalization, the normalized counts remain, category
and code are overridden to reporter-contract/cleanup-failed, and the signature is recomputed with
that override before receipt append. A missing or stale normalized signature is never an
alternative valid tuple. Signature-construction failure before a valid normalized result discards
partial normalized values, records `schema_failed` / `failure_signature_contract_failed`, and
keeps the signature null.

The in-memory adversarial reference model covers pass, missing, process-stream 1 MiB + 1,
reporter 16 MiB + 1, malformed JSON,
unknown path, duplicate path, count mismatch, unknown field, exit contradiction, assertion-level,
file/suite-level and worker/process-level failure, stable signature under changed secret messages,
and absence of raw secret material. This is V0 candidate validation only and is not an Attempt.
The focused correction validator additionally covers every lifecycle state, all seven categories,
all five normalization statuses, all 31 result fields, all 46 precedence checks, the 12 multi-fault
fixtures, 13 Cleanup-V2 timing fields, all 64 signal reattestation tuples, eight focused signal and
six termination-partition fixtures, rejected removal without binding, cleanup override/re-signing
and invalid key, `undefined`, extra, overlap and stale-signature tuples.

### Candidate gate

No executor may materialize any fresh path, create a worktree, install dependencies, invoke a
mapped gate or write an Attempt receipt until an independent reviewer returns `APPROVED` on this
exact candidate and the approved six-file ADO delta is published exactly. Review findings require
correction and renewed independent review. The conditional standing authorization reaches at most
one exact R3 Attempt-12 run after those gates; it does not reach Hardware or any Product/Human V5
gate.

## 12. Attempt-12 terminal synchronization

Attempt 12 executed once and is consumed fail-closed. Executable source/tree remained
`a0359a87fd1738c8493929a1661cbbc7adb3c07c` /
`102c913e264bd0ccce1d085db1c50bd407f7d4a4`; publication commit/tree were
`db1fc8891d03753b2266957d45137e1817e46156` /
`4fa39b6261e5f856d8f982bedee1ec843b371ed6`. The immutable evidence directory is
`/Users/timbartz/Dokumente/GitHub/taptime-local-evidence/da5-v5-harness/attempt12-a0359a87-710d46dc`.
It contains exactly three mode-`0444` files under mode `0555`:

- receipt: 73,329 bytes, SHA-256
  `eae30ed497d64392c26dbd670b2118b5396bfbf6d63da4e7fffea59b45f26f21`;
- precleanup snapshot: 45,249 bytes, SHA-256
  `e6c040d2db7943b3f8782b0734bdcacc836b2a28d610ce6dde1ee54e4926b864`;
- evidence manifest: 966 bytes, SHA-256
  `7acc9cd3848e80a760edc77f2aa005b38329b62136a92ebb1bd29153374707d8`.

The exact 45-record distribution is 34 `passed`, two `failed` and nine `omitted`. Gate 32
`V2_SYNTHETIC_TEST` is the first failure; `FINALIZE` is the second. Gate 32 has one exact mapped
exit-1 process and a fully normalized 118,443-byte reporter. Expected and observed file
membership are exactly 13/13 with digest
`6d3d0d28585a65d8e1357716285896176549416262b3fdba5e5a88ff4966716f`.
It records 13 files (11 passed, two failed), 42 suites (38 passed, four failed), and 297 tests
(273 passed, six failed, 18 skipped). The only justified category is
`assertion_result_or_test_hook_failure_ambiguous`, code `vitest_reported_failure`. Raw reporter,
stdout and stderr are absent. No narrower test, hook or Product cause may be inferred.

Independent execution/evidence review returned `CHANGES REQUIRED`:

1. P2: mapped `CLEANUP[0]` lacked `cleanup_state_before`, `actual_root_results`,
   `actual_registration_result` and `cleanup_state_after`; mapped `POSTCLEANUP[0]` was absent.
2. P2: the actual 51,266-byte in-memory executor had no prepublished independently verifiable
   artifact anchor; its self-reported hash is insufficient.
3. P2: Gate 32 did not persist the canonical `failing_files` inputs — allowlisted path,
   `failed_assertion_count` and `failed_identifier_sha256` — so its legacy failure signature cannot
   be independently recomputed after raw reporter removal.
4. P3: the earlier 35/1/9 distribution was wrong and is corrected here to 34/2/9.

### Collect-safe, hard-stop and omission model

All 45 gate records remain explicit and ordered. `PRECLEANUP_SNAPSHOT`, `CLEANUP`, `POSTCLEANUP`
and `FINALIZE` always execute. The decision classes are disjoint:

- **hard stop:** Gates 1–10 and Gate 27; any non-start/signal; worker/process/infrastructure
  anomaly; provenance, identity, mapping, argv, env, tool, source, publication, schema, bounds,
  parser, raw-disclosure, reporter-contract, symlink, special-file, output-root, output-integrity,
  cleanup or postcleanup anomaly at any gate. At most one closed hard-stop object is stored.
  Remaining Gates through 41 become `not_run_hard_stop`.
- **collectable quality failure:** only a fully attestable exit/nonzero or closed predicate failure
  at Gates 11–26, 28–32, 34, 38, 39 or 40 after exact output/provenance attestation. Exactly one
  entry per gate and at most 25 entries are allowed. A second entry, overflow, duplicate, wrong
  order, invalid enum, stale signature or extra field is a hard stop.
- **dependency omission:** only a named direct prerequisite or required input that is non-green,
  unavailable or ambiguous makes its dependent `dependency_omitted`. The record has null exit,
  an empty process list, sorted direct dependency IDs and their exact gate-result signatures.
  Unrelated downstream gates still run.
- **pass/carry:** a carry repeats the original green gate-result signature and proves the exact
  tracked input/source binding unchanged. Carry drift is a hard stop.

Each quality entry has exactly `gate_number`, `gate_id`, closed `quality_kind`, closed
`failure_code`, nullable `process_index`, the closed termination tuple and
`gate_result_signature`. The list is unique and gate-number sorted. Final evidence records
`entry_count`, SHA-256 of compact canonical list, at most one hard-stop object, omission count and
digest, and `raw_output_preserved:false`. Closed quality kinds are `build_nonzero`,
`test_nonzero`, `typecheck_nonzero`, `typecheck_membership_false`, `node_check_nonzero`,
`metafile_predicate_false` and `talkback_predicate_false`.

The closed termination tuple is exactly `not_started|exit|signal`. `process_exit_code` is nonnull
only for `exit`; `signal_category` is nonnull only for `signal`. Raw signal name/number and a
synthesized `128+signal` exit are forbidden. A signal is always a hard stop, never a collectable
quality result.

### Exact dependency decisions for Gates 32–41

- Gate 32 execution-depends only on green Gate 27; earlier independent quality results do not omit
  it.
- Gate 33 carries Gate 31 only when Gate 31 is green and its inputs remain unchanged.
- Gate 34 is independent of Gate 32 and Gate 33 and execution-depends only on green Gate 27. Its
  second mapped child runs only after the first child is green; first-child quality failure omits
  that child without blocking independent Gates 35 and 36.
- Gates 35 and 36 carry Gates 28 and 30 respectively under unchanged-input proof.
- Gate 37 carries Gate 34 only when the complete Gate-34 result is green.
- Gate 38 requires green Gate 34 and Gate 37 plus the exact `dist/da5V5Main.js` identity.
- Gate 39 is independent of Gate 38. It runs only if Gate-34 esbuild ran successfully after its
  green first child and the exact metafile/output identity is proven.
- Gate 40 requires the exact Gate-34 output and green Gate-39 verified graph. It preserves the
  current TalkBack-closure predicate unchanged.
- Gate 41 requires every required Gate 1–40 result green or validly carried, zero collected
  failures, no hard stop, Gates 38–40 green, and no deferred reporter cleanup or residue.

Gate 39 requires exactly the existing 14 allowlisted Synthetic `dist` outputs and exact graph
inputs `apps/synthetic-android-e2e/src/da5V5Main.ts`,
`apps/synthetic-android-e2e/src/Da5V5AdbController.ts` and
`apps/mobile/scripts/da5V5AndroidDevice.mjs`. It rejects all runtime references to
`@expo/expo-modules-macros-plugin`, `expo-modules-jsi` and `expo-modules-core`. Gate 40 additionally
binds the exact reviewed source hashes, graph edges from main to controller to mobile device

### Gate-32 reproducible safe failure evidence

The bounded Attempt-12 reporter/schema/allowlist contract remains authoritative, with one
addition: every normalized result persists canonical `failing_files`. Each entry contains only an
exact source-allowlisted repository-relative path, `failed_assertion_count`, and
`failed_identifier_sha256`. The identifier digest hashes the UTF-8 compact JSON multiset of
unsigned-byte-sorted individual SHA-256 values for NFC `[ancestorTitles...,title]`; duplicates
remain. File entries are exact allowlist-byte sorted. The executor derives the signature schema
from the loaded adapted map and requires the exact V1 literal
`DA5_V5_ATTEMPT13_VITEST_FAILURE_SIGNATURE_V1`; it does not substitute a locally invented schema.
The normalized signature hashes the closed category/code, termination tuple,
expected/observed membership digests, exact counts and these persisted entries. Messages, failure
text, stack, timestamps, durations, metadata, tags, arbitrary paths, reporter bytes and
stdout/stderr never enter evidence.

The complete normalized object is closed as exactly 31 result fields plus a separate canonical
`failing_files` envelope and exactly 13 binding fields. File/suite/test partitions, failing
assertion sums, status/code/category precedence, gate/kind/process index, source membership,
signature lifecycle and terminal binding rules are validated cross-field. A source byte-count or
digest mismatch maps to the existing closed `unexpected_file_path` failure code. A preflight
failure remains schema-legal `not_started`; a process that never starts has `processes:[]` and an
explicit gate termination tuple. The inventory attests exactly two allowed roots,
`node_modules` and the reporter root, while allowing mutation only in the exact identity-bound
reporter root and restoring its before-state.

Only a completely normalized assertion/test-hook or suite/hook/collection/transform/config result
may be collected. Process-not-started, signal, worker/process/infrastructure result, exit/result
contradiction, reporter/contract error, bounds/parser/schema failure or reporter cleanup residue is
a hard stop. Reporter raw content is removed by exact identity before receipt; failure to do so
hard-stops and defers only to the already exact-bound checkout cleanup.

### Cleanup and artifact closure

`CLEANUP[0]` and `POSTCLEANUP[0]` each retain their one mapped process record, including a
safely forbidden launch with closed `not_started` termination. No additional Git probe child is
permitted. Both child records carry `cleanup_state_before`, `actual_root_results`,
`actual_registration_result` and `cleanup_state_after`; POSTCLEANUP additionally carries
`successful_artifact_preservation` and `evidence_preservation`. Each cleanup state has exactly
eight fields; nullable identities have exactly four fields and worktree mappings exactly six.
The fixed root-result array contains exactly five exact nine-field objects in order `checkout`,
`cache`, `logs`, `config`, `failure_only_artifact`; the registration result has exactly 13 fields.

Worktree removal occurs only after exact checkout, registration and singleton worktree-list
mapping revalidation. A vanished captured root is deferred to cleanup and is not rewritten as
`not_created`. Missing, mismatched or ambiguous identity never authorizes removal. Bound success
is exactly `bound -> cleanup_applied -> cleanup_complete`. Unbound, partial and all 56
mismatch/ambiguity tuples launch zero removal and enter or retain `cleanup_residue`.
`cleanup_residue` remains absorbing: CLEANUP and POSTCLEANUP may only retain it as named terminal
evidence with sequence plus one and zero removal attempts; FINALIZE makes no transition, copies
both exact committed records, sets both completion flags false and remains `FAIL_CLOSED`.
POSTCLEANUP derives fresh root, registration and mapping observations from its mapped output and
filesystem state rather than aliasing Cleanup observations.

Gate 41 stages the two exact success files in memory only. The external success artifact is
materialized only in `FINALIZE`, after complete Cleanup/Postcleanup, an empty failure ledger, no
hard stop and all required gates green/carried. An ordinary failure requires artifact `null` and
exact artifact-path absence. Artifact observation and rollback are total: null or partial
transaction state, a thrown observation, or a parent/creation/removal mismatch authorizes zero
removal and records truthful residue. A creation-bound descriptor is legal only for a proved
created object with nonnull creation identity. If a caught Finalize publication fault leaves an
artifact whose exact parent/creation binding cannot be revalidated, removal is forbidden and
Finalize records only the closed residue descriptor; no success artifact is claimed. Before
Finalize, committed receipt records are deep-copied and frozen;
Finalize derives and validates its cleanup copies, final state, completion flags and manifest
status from those immutable records. Its common PASS/FAIL transaction stages artifact/evidence
and modes, commits the receipt last, and rolls back the staged/truncated prefix for each caught
write or chmod failure. This is catchable-exception rollback, not a crash-atomic guarantee.
Prejournal initialization likewise records the receipt file identity before fallible realpath or
readback operations. A failed rollback is never suppressed and never clears the identity/handle
bindings still needed for terminal disposition; only exact root, receipt and entry state may be
removed, otherwise the closed outcome remains disclosure-safe and authorizes zero removal.

Evidence remains exactly receipt and evidence manifest plus the fixed-name snapshot when that
entry is present. The snapshot manifest field is exactly one of: `null` only after proved absence;
the existing fully bound `{name,bytes,sha256}` object; or the closed residue
`{name,status,removal_attempted:false}` with status exactly
`present_unbound|present_ambiguous`. A residue may disclose no bytes, digest, content, raw path or
identity details; it is never chmodded or removed, forces `evidence_preserved:false`, zero removal
and `FAIL_CLOSED`, and is retained in the truthful exact three-entry Gate-45 set. A failed Gate-42
write rolls back only by exact identity. The final two- or three-entry set is frozen only to the
extent safely bound with the 45th record.
